# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccountAssignment

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAssignment.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| instanceId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":64} |
| estimatedLoad | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "estimatedLoad": 4,
  "instanceId": "example",
  "role": "example"
}
```

## AccountAvailability

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAvailability.json), input validation.

Type: ["available","near_limit","exhausted","logged_out","unknown"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"near_limit"
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

## AccountEnvKey

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountEnvKey.json), input validation.

Type: ["CODEX_HOME","CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR","XDG_DATA_HOME","XDG_CONFIG_HOME","XDG_STATE_HOME","XDG_CACHE_HOME","CURSOR_DATA_DIR"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"CODEX_HOME"
```

## AccountQuota

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountQuota.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| auth | yes | ["logged_in","logged_out","unknown"] |  |
| observedAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| windows | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"maxProperties":32,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 32 quota windows."} |
| blockers | no | object | {"default":{},"properties":{"overflow":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"limitError":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"}}} |
| usage | yes | object | {"properties":{"inputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"outputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"costUsd":{"type":"number","minimum":0}}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "auth": "logged_out",
  "blockers": {},
  "observedAt": 7,
  "usage": {
    "inputTokens": 1,
    "outputTokens": 8
  },
  "windows": {}
}
```

## AccountSummary

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountSummary.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| isolation | no | `"unsupported"` |  |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| quota | yes | [AccountQuota.json](schema/AccountQuota.json) |  |
| availability | yes | [AccountAvailability.json](schema/AccountAvailability.json) |  |

Example:

```json

  "acpAgentId": "example",
  "availability": "near_limit",
  "homeStrategy": "default_cli",
  "id": "example",
  "instanceId": "example",
  "label": "example",
  "loginRevision": "example",
  "provider": "opencode",
  "quota": {
    "auth": "logged_out",
    "blockers": {
      "overflow": true
    },
    "observedAt": 3,
    "usage": {},
    "windows": {}
  }
}
```

## AcpSessionSupport

[JSON Schema](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| capabilities | yes | object | {"properties":{"resume":{"type":"boolean"},"imageInput":{"type":"boolean"},"planMode":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["resume","imageInput","planMode"]} |
| mcp | yes | ["http","stdio","unavailable"] |  |
| modelSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| modeSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| subagentSessions | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| coverage | yes | ["source_profile","generic","legacy"] |  |
| visibility | yes | `"limited"` |  |

```json
  },

  "visibility": "limited"
}
```

## Agent

[JSON Schema](schema/Agent.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| childThreadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| parentId | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AgentId.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| origin | yes | [AgentOrigin.json](schema/AgentOrigin.json) |  |
| lineage | no | [ThreadLineage.json](schema/ThreadLineage.json) |  |
| native | yes | [NativeRef.json](schema/NativeRef.json) |  |
| fidelity | yes | [AgentFidelity.json](schema/AgentFidelity.json) |  |
| spawnedBy | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ItemId.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| name | no | string |  |
| role | no | string |  |
| model | no | string |  |
| cwd | yes | string |  |
| status | yes | [AgentStatus.json](schema/AgentStatus.json) |  |
| background | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| endedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "background": true,
  "childThreadId": "example",
  "createdAt": 9,
  "cwd": "example",
  "endedAt": 6,
  "fidelity": "summary",
  "id": "example",
  "lineage": {
    "lossy": true,
    "mode": "native",
    "parentAgentId": "example",
    "parentThreadId": "example",
    "point": {
      "runId": "example",
      "type": "turn"
    }
  },
  "native": {
    "forkedFromNativeId": "example",
    "provider": "cursor"
  },
  "origin": "ace",
  "parentId": null,
  "spawnedBy": null,
  "status": {
    "on": "human",
    "refs": [],
    "state": "blocked",
    "until": 8
  },
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

| capabilities | yes | [BrowserBackendCapabilities.json](schema/BrowserBackendCapabilities.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "capabilities": {
    "cdp": true,
    "controllerLease": true,
    "downloads": true,
    "permissions": true,
    "targets": true
  },
  "credential": "2ceddef086bc0889b2d6a9ed7969c2e0629e9f48321fb9006627ef44eb490333",
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "browser.backend.register",
  "version": 1
}
```

Example:

```json

  "data": "T7YTZ00IIrIRUsLJcFqUWN6bH88Gsbk1427=",
  "offset": 3,
  "op": "upload.chunk",
  "uploadId": "rEBC"
}
```

### Variant 4

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"upload.commit"` |  |
| uploadId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"} |

Example:

```json
  "op": "upload.commit",

}
```

### Variant 5

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

```json
}
```

| result | yes | union | {"oneOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"error"},"code":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ContextErrorCode.json"},"message":{"type":"string"}},"required":["kind","code","message"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"upload"},"uploadId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w-]+$"},"offset":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"bytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["kind","uploadId","offset","bytes"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"attachment"},"attachment":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Attachment.json"}},"required":["kind","attachment"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"attachments"},"attachments":{"maxItems":256,"type":"array","items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Attachment.json"}}},"required":["kind","attachments"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"mentions"},"entries":{"type":"array","items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ResolvedMention.json"}},"diagnostics":{"type":"array","items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ContextDiagnostic.json"}}},"required":["kind","entries","diagnostics"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"completion"},"paths":{"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}},"required":["kind","paths"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"ok"}},"required":["kind"]}]} |

## DeviceInventory

[JSON Schema](schema/DeviceInventory.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| devices | yes | array | {"maxItems":1024,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AppDevice.json"}} |
| issues | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":2,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DeviceFailure.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "devices": []
}
```

## DeviceOperation

[JSON Schema](schema/DeviceOperation.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"enable"` |  |
| enabled | yes | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "enabled": true,
  "op": "enable"
}
```

### Variant 2

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"list"` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "list"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"states"` |  |

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"approve"` |  |

| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| allowed | yes | boolean |  |

```json
{
  "allowed": true,
```

## DeviceScope

[JSON Schema](schema/DeviceScope.json), input validation.

Type: ["read","operate","admin","desktop"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"read"
```

## DeviceServerMessage

[JSON Schema](schema/DeviceServerMessage.json), input validation.

### devices.result

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"devices.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| ok | yes | boolean |  |
| data | no | any JSON value |  |
| error | no | [DeviceFailure.json](schema/DeviceFailure.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "ok": false,
  "requestId": "eSTRvmd",
  "type": "devices.result"
}
```

### devices.state

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"devices.state"` |  |
| state | yes | [DeviceState.json](schema/DeviceState.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "state": {
    "approved": false,
    "controller": "none",
    "device": {
      "id": "ios:2-aD8AbabC62c3e1BfBbfa5Bb8B76c3eBc6c",
      "name": "example",
      "platform": "ios",
      "state": "unauthorized"
    },
    "enabled": true,
    "leaseExpiresAt": 0,
    "lifecycle": "live"
  },
  "type": "devices.state"
}
```

### devices.logs

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"devices.logs"` |  |
| deviceId | yes | [AppDeviceId.json](schema/AppDeviceId.json) |  |
| sequence | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| lines | yes | array | {"maxItems":64,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096}} |
| dropped | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

Example:

```json
{
  "deviceId": "android:WZ0GgojP8tp6pfA9EC3UFA0FMQ-FnumQjb.lPM4SA3cIKHtwagjW3xseXJfzsXZrY3M0s9yjFzzLWT9KV5JJbjDDa4IaYAWDTaXqi",
  "dropped": 3,
  "lines": [],
  "sequence": 0,
  "type": "devices.logs"
}
```

## DeviceSettings

[JSON Schema](schema/DeviceSettings.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| appearance | no | ["light","dark"] |  |
| location | no | object | {"properties":{"latitude":{"type":"number","minimum":-90,"maximum":90},"longitude":{"type":"number","minimum":-180,"maximum":180}},"required":["latitude","longitude"]} |
| locale | no | string | {"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z]{2,8}(?:[-_][a-zA-Z0-9]{2,8}){0,2}$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "locale": "vKhzLkd"
}
```

## DeviceState

[JSON Schema](schema/DeviceState.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| device | yes | [AppDevice.json](schema/AppDevice.json) |  |
| enabled | yes | boolean |  |
| approved | yes | boolean |  |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| lifecycle | yes | ["idle","starting","live","stopping","failed"] |  |
| streamId | no | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| controller | yes | ["none","human","agent"] |  |
| leaseExpiresAt | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| error | no | [DeviceFailure.json](schema/DeviceFailure.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "approved": false,
  "controller": "agent",
  "device": {
    "id": "android:U-VvP7NCox3D11ngB6MwuCYJ08HFOWCL-5eVcD1tS1Uhynm00GzPNfSThjpip8dWWOSnHcNOQn3qFHJSXbqIXlG088VqsaHv2-60Sio_GHS.MEB6YWrvW071M9DE3IRi.lhLx7tkaMj7793AwtPuUR_",
    "name": "example",
    "platform": "android",
    "state": "unauthorized"
  },
  "enabled": false,
  "lifecycle": "idle",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

## DiagnosticsHealth

[JSON Schema](schema/DiagnosticsHealth.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| at | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| eventLoop | yes | object | {"properties":{"meanMs":{"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]},"p99Ms":{"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]},"maxMs":{"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]}},"required":["meanMs","p99Ms","maxMs"]} |
| memory | yes | object | {"properties":{"rssBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"heapUsedBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"heapTotalBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["rssBytes","heapUsedBytes","heapTotalBytes"]} |
| openHandles | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| sqlite | yes | object | {"properties":{"pageBytes":{"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]},"walBytes":{"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]}},"required":["pageBytes","walBytes"]} |
| activeSessions | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| queues | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"maxProperties":64,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 64 queue entries."} |
| logs | yes | object | {"properties":{"dropped":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"failed":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"queued":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["dropped","failed","queued"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "activeSessions": null,
  "at": 5,
  "eventLoop": {
    "maxMs": 4,
    "meanMs": null,
    "p99Ms": 7
  },
  "logs": {
    "dropped": 9,
    "failed": 7,
    "queued": 6
  },
  "memory": {
    "heapTotalBytes": 1,
    "heapUsedBytes": 8,
    "rssBytes": 2
  },
  "openHandles": 3,
  "queues": {},
  "sqlite": {
    "pageBytes": null,
    "walBytes": null
  }
}
```

## EventId

[JSON Schema](schema/EventId.json), input validation.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"example"
```

## ExecutionOptions

[JSON Schema](schema/ExecutionOptions.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: At most 32 provider option entries.

Type: object. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
{}
```

## ExecutionSelection

[JSON Schema](schema/ExecutionSelection.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| options | no | [ExecutionOptions.json](schema/ExecutionOptions.json) | {"default":{}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "instanceId": "example",
  "model": "example",
  "provider": "acp"
}
```

## ExecutionSource

[JSON Schema](schema/ExecutionSource.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| nativeSessionId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| selection | yes | [ExecutionSelection.json](schema/ExecutionSelection.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "nativeSessionId": "example",
  "selection": {
    "model": "example",
    "options": {},
    "provider": "antigravity"
  }
}
```

## FileChange

[JSON Schema](schema/FileChange.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| path | yes | string |  |
| kind | yes | ["add","update","delete","move"] |  |
| movePath | no | string |  |
| diff | no | string |  |
| oldText | no | string,null |  |
| newText | no | string |  |

Example:

```json

  "kind": "add",
  "path": "example"
}
```

| op | yes | `"upload.commit"` |  |

## PortableHandoff

[JSON Schema](schema/PortableHandoff.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `1` |  |
| sourceThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| origin | no | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"backend":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["provider"]} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| lossy | yes | `true` |  |
| policy | yes | `"recent-complete-items"` |  |
| excerpts | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"citation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffCitation.json"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["citation","text"]}} |
| omittedItems | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| history | yes | object | {"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"items.page"},"tool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff"},"chunkTool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff_chunk","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff_chunk"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"before":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"limit":{"type":"number","const":50}},"required":["type","threadId","before","limit"]} |
| limitations | yes | `"Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments."` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "excerpts": [],
  "history": {
    "before": 4,
    "limit": 50,
    "threadId": "example",
    "type": "items.page"
  },
  "limitations": "Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments.",
  "lossy": true,
  "omittedItems": 5,
  "policy": "recent-complete-items",
  "sourceThreadId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 2,
  "version": 1
}
```

## PreviewLaunch

[JSON Schema](schema/PreviewLaunch.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Choose exactly one executable, or an attach URL without an executable or autoPort.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| name | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| command | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| runtimeExecutable | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| args | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096}} |
| cwd | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| env | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| port | no | [PreviewPort.json](schema/PreviewPort.json) |  |
| autoPort | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| url | no | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":4096,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |

Example:

```json
{
  "command": "node",
  "name": "example"
}
```

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references, id equal to instanceId, default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "acpAgentId": "local:agent",
  "env": {},
  "homeDir": "/example/home",
  "homeStrategy": "default_cli",
  "id": "installed:default",
  "installationId": "installed",
  "instanceId": "installed:default",
  "label": "Local agent",
  "provider": "acp"
}
```

| noFinalNewline | no | `true` |  |

```json
  "before": [],
  "lines": [

  ]
}
```

| comments | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":20,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"position":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ReviewPosition.json"},"text":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096},"suggestion":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192},"excerpt":{"type":"string","maxLength":2048}},"required":["id","position","text","excerpt"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "comments": [
    {
      "excerpt": "example",
      "id": "example",
      "position": {
        "end": 1,
        "file": "example.ts",
        "side": "new",
        "start": 1
      },
      "text": "example"
    }
  ],
  "kind": "review-fix",
  "requestId": "example",
  "sessionId": "example",
  "source": {
    "from": {
      "kind": "commit",
      "ref": "example"
    },
    "to": {
      "kind": "working-tree"
    },
    "workspaceId": "example",
    "worktree": "example"
  },
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

## ReviewPath

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewPath.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Repository-relative path without control characters, backslashes, drive prefixes, empty, dot or parent segments.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"example"
```

## ReviewPosition

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewPosition.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: end >= start and end - start < 100.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| file | yes | [ReviewPath.json](schema/ReviewPath.json) |  |
| side | yes | ["old","new"] |  |
| start | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":10000000} |
| end | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":10000000} |

Example:

```json
{
  "end": 1,
  "file": "example.ts",
  "side": "new",
  "start": 1
}
```

| text | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |

## ScreenCapabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `2` |  |
| platform | yes | ["macos","windows","linux-x11","linux-wayland"] |  |
| capture | yes | object | {"properties":{"windows":{"type":"boolean"},"displays":{"type":"boolean"},"changeDriven":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["windows","displays","changeDriven"]} |
| input | yes | object | {"properties":{"pointer":{"type":"boolean"},"keyboard":{"type":"boolean"},"scroll":{"type":"boolean"},"text":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["pointer","keyboard","scroll","text"]} |
| uiTree | yes | boolean |  |
| semanticActions | yes | array | {"maxItems":6,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["press","focus","setValue","scroll","expand","select"]}} |
| codecs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["jpeg","h264"]}} |
| permissions | yes | object | {"properties":{"screen":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]},"input":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]}},"required":["screen","input"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capture": {
    "changeDriven": true,
    "displays": true,
    "windows": false
  },
  "codecs": [
    "jpeg"
  ],
  "input": {
    "keyboard": false,
    "pointer": false,
    "scroll": true,
    "text": false
  },
  "permissions": {
    "input": "prompt",
    "screen": "prompt"
  },
  "platform": "linux-wayland",
  "semanticActions": [],
  "uiTree": true,
  "version": 2
}
```

| status | no | [SearchState.json](schema/SearchState.json) |  |
| agentId | no | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| kind | no | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": 1,
  "agentId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

Type: ["new","working","waiting","needs_you","done","failed","unresponsive"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"new"
```

## SearchStatus

[JSON Schema](schema/SearchStatus.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| indexedSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| headSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| pending | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| indexWrites | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| generation | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| ready | yes | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "generation": 8,
  "headSeq": 3,
  "indexWrites": 6,
  "indexedSeq": 1,
  "pending": 4,
  "ready": false
}
```

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |
| agentTree | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":1000} |
| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |
| equivalentApiCost | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| quotaAccount | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "from": "2026-10-01",
  "to": "2026-10-02"
}
```

## WebPushSubscription

[JSON Schema](schema/WebPushSubscription.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| endpoint | yes | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |
| p256dh | yes | string | {"pattern":"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{87}$"} |
| auth | yes | string | {"pattern":"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{22}$"} |

```json
  "auth": "5v0v8pGoZZ52xXy29xqtSe",
  "endpoint": "https://example.invalid/",
```
